([A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E](https://github.com/A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E/blob/HEAD/dotclaude/agents/codex-claude-workflow-coordinator.md) · ★3)
- **codex-cli**: Execute OpenAI Codex CLI (GPT-5.2) for code analysis. Use when you need Codex's GPT-5.2 perspective on code. ([centminmod/my-claude-code-setup](https://github.com/centminmod/my-claude-code-setup/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/codex-cli.md) · ★2.6k · 3 copies)
- **codex-cli**: * Node.js 20.6+ * The `webcmd` npm CLI, installed globally or in the project * Codex CLI (`codex`), installed and authenticated * A browser runtime; confirm with `webcmd doctor` before browser work ([agentrhq/webcmd](https://github.com/agentrhq/webcmd/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/codex-cli.md) · ★2.2k)
- **codex-cli**: **Status: machine-configured ◐** — config written by `watch-skill setup` on a real machine; `codex mcp list` shows watch-skill enabled and the server command answers MCP `initialize`. A full codex chat session was not run. ([oxbshw/watch-skill](https://github.com/oxbshw/watch-skill/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/codex-cli.md) · ★379)
- **codex-cli**: Wraps Codex CLI (OpenAI) for token-optimized invocation from Claude Code or any AI harness. Captures --help, caches subcommands, validates flags, post-processes output. Use when Claude Code needs to delegate work to or introspect Codex CLI. ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/blob/HEAD/plugins/cli-wrapper/agents/codex-cli.md) · ★14)
- **codex-cli-fallback**: FALLBACK GPT lane via the raw Codex CLI. The primary codex lane is the official codex plugin's codex:codex-rescue agent - use this one ONLY when that plugin is not installed AND the Code-Ultra config sets codex_fallback raw-cli. Drives `codex exec` non-interactively on a five-part spec; verifies the result itself; reports with evidence. Fails loudly when codex is unavailable - never substitutes itself. ([InverseAltruism/Code-Ultra](https://github.com/InverseAltruism/Code-Ultra/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-cli-fallback.md))

- **codex-cloud-environment**: This is the canonical operating policy for Cloud tasks in this repository. Follow the current system, developer and user instructions, then the applicable repository rules. Old chats, copied prompts, archived runbooks and machine names are evidence to verify, not a second source of authority. ([BigSimmo/Database](https://github.com/BigSimmo/Database/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/codex-cloud-environment.md))

- **codex-code-reviewer**: Semantic code review using codex CLI tool. Reviews files against project-specific rules aligned with TypeScript patterns. ([jagreehal/jagreehal-claude-skills](https://github.com/jagreehal/jagreehal-claude-skills/blob/HEAD/code-review/agents/codex-code-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **codex-code-reviewer**: Use this agent when you want to get a code review from OpenAI's Codex CLI (codex-cli) to obtain perspectives that differ from Claude's analysis. This is particularly useful for getting a second opinion on code quality, potential bugs, or alternative approaches. The agent should be invoked after writing a significant piece of code or when explicitly requested for a Codex-based review.\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: The user has just finished implementing a new function and wants a different perspective on the code.\nuser: "isHoliday 関数を実装したので、Codex にレビューしてもらいたい"\nassistant: "Codex CLI を使ってコードレビューを実行しま… ([model-geek/japanese-holidays](https://github.com/model-geek/japanese-holidays/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/codex-code-reviewer.md) · ★3)

- **codex-coder**: GPT-5.5/Codex coding assistant. Use for isolated code generation, refactoring, test writing, and CLI tool tasks. Optimized for outcome-first short prompts. Read it only when the &gt; calling step has confirmed its Codex toggle is `"true"` and the codex ([maxritter/pilot-shell](https://github.com/maxritter/pilot-shell/blob/HEAD/pilot/agents/codex-companion-protocol.md) · ★2.1k)
